    52 000011A1 CF                  <1>     iret
    53                              <1> 
    54                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    55                              <1> ; AH=01h: Get status of last operation.
    56                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    57                              <1> .get_last_status:
    58 000011A2 2E8A26[3B11]        <1>     mov ah, [cs:int13_last_status]
    59 000011A7 84E4                <1>     test ah, ah
    60 000011A9 7402                <1>     jz .gls_ok
    61 000011AB F9                  <1>     stc
    62 000011AC CF                  <1>     iret
    63                              <1> .gls_ok:
    64 000011AD F8                  <1>     clc
    65 000011AE CF                  <1>     iret
    66                              <1> 
    67                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    68                              <1> ; AH=00h: Reset disk system.
    69                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    70                              <1> .reset_disk:
    71 000011AF 2EC606[3B11]00      <1>     mov byte [cs:int13_last_status], 0
    72 000011B5 30E4                <1>     xor ah, ah                  ; Success
    73 000011B7 F8                  <1>     clc
    74 000011B8 CF                  <1>     iret
    75                              <1> 
    76                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    77                              <1> ; AH=02h: Read sectors using CHS.
    78                              <1> ;   AL = sector count, CH = cylinder low, CL[5:0] = sector, CL[7:6]+CH = cyl,
    79                              <1> ;   DH = head, DL = drive, ES:BX = buffer.
    80                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    81                              <1> .read_sectors_chs:
    82 000011B9 6650                <1>     push eax
    83 000011BB 6653                <1>     push ebx
    84 000011BD 6651                <1>     push ecx
    85 000011BF 6652                <1>     push edx
    86 000011C1 6657                <1>     push edi
    87 000011C3 06                  <1>     push es
    88                              <1> 
    89                              <1>     ; Support first HDD (0x80) and virtual El Torito drive (0xE0).
    90 000011C4 80FA80              <1>     cmp dl, 0x80
    91 000011C7 7408                <1>     je .chs_drive_ok
    92 000011C9 80FAE0              <1>     cmp dl, 0xE0
    93 000011CC 7403                <1>     je .chs_drive_ok
    94 000011CE E99F00              <1>     jmp .chs_error
    95                              <1> 
    96                              <1> .chs_drive_ok:
    97                              <1> 
    98 000011D1 2E803E[BE09]00      <1>     cmp byte [cs:ide_master_present], 0
    99 000011D7 0F849500            <1>     je .chs_error
   100                              <1> 
   101                              <1>     ; Convert CHS to LBA: LBA = (C * H + h) * S + (s - 1)
   102                              <1>     ; where H = total heads, S = sectors per track.
   103 000011DB 660FB6C5            <1>     movzx eax, ch               ; Cylinder low 8 bits
   104 000011DF 88CB                <1>     mov bl, cl
   105 000011E1 C0EB06              <1>     shr bl, 6                   ; Cylinder high 2 bits
   106 000011E4 660FB6DB            <1>     movzx ebx, bl
   107 000011E8 66C1E308            <1>     shl ebx, 8
   108 000011EC 6609D8              <1>     or eax, ebx                 ; EAX = cylinder
   109                              <1> 
   110                              <1>     ; CHS geometry for conversion.
   111                              <1>     ; HDD: use IDENTIFY geometry.
   112                              <1>     ; CD no-emulation: expose synthetic geometry (64 heads, 32 spt)
   113                              <1>     ; consistent with AH=08.
   114 000011EF 80FAE0              <1>     cmp dl, 0xE0
   115 000011F2 7508                <1>     jne .chs_geom_hdd
   116 000011F4 66BB40000000        <1>     mov ebx, 64
   117 000011FA EB07                <1>     jmp .chs_heads_ok
   118                              <1> .chs_geom_hdd:
   119 000011FC 2E660FB71E[C109]    <1>     movzx ebx, word [cs:ide_master_heads]
   120                              <1> .chs_heads_ok:
   121 00001203 66F7E3              <1>     mul ebx                     ; EAX = C * H
   122 00001206 660FB6DE            <1>     movzx ebx, dh               ; Head
   123 0000120A 6601D8              <1>     add eax, ebx                ; EAX = C * H + h
   124                              <1> 
   125 0000120D 80FAE0              <1>     cmp dl, 0xE0
   126 00001210 7508                <1>     jne .chs_spt_hdd
   127 00001212 66BB20000000        <1>     mov ebx, 32
   128 00001218 EB07                <1>     jmp .chs_spt_ok
   129                              <1> .chs_spt_hdd:
   130 0000121A 2E660FB71E[C309]    <1>     movzx ebx, word [cs:ide_master_spt]
   131                              <1> .chs_spt_ok:
   132 00001221 66F7E3              <1>     mul ebx                     ; EAX = (C*H + h) * S
   133                              <1> 
   134 00001224 88CB                <1>     mov bl, cl
   135 00001226 80E33F              <1>     and bl, 0x3F                ; Sector (1-based)
   136 00001229 FECB                <1>     dec bl
   137 0000122B 660FB6DB            <1>     movzx ebx, bl
   138 0000122F 6601D8              <1>     add eax, ebx                ; EAX = LBA
   139                              <1> 
   140                              <1>     ; Now read using LBA. Sector count was in AL on entry.
   141                              <1>     ; We saved original regs, so recover sector count.
   142 00001232 07                  <1>     pop es
   143 00001233 06                  <1>     push es
   144 00001234 67668B4C2414        <1>     mov ecx, [esp + 20]         ; Original EAX (AL = count)
   145 0000123A 660FB6C9            <1>     movzx ecx, cl               ; ECX = sector count
   146                              <1>     ; EAX = LBA, ECX = count, ES:BX was set by caller.
   147                              <1>     ; Recover original BX from stack.
   148 0000123E 67668B7C2410        <1>     mov edi, [esp + 16]         ; Original EBX
   149 00001244 6681E7FFFF0000      <1>     and edi, 0xFFFF             ; DI = buffer offset (original BX)
   150                              <1> 
   151 0000124B E8CB03              <1>     call ide_read_sectors
   152 0000124E 7215                <1>     jc .chs_error_pop
   153                              <1> 
   154 00001250 07                  <1>     pop es
   155 00001251 665F                <1>     pop edi
   156 00001253 665A                <1>     pop edx
   157 00001255 6659                <1>     pop ecx
   158 00001257 665B                <1>     pop ebx
   159 00001259 6658                <1>     pop eax
   160                              <1>     ; AL = sectors read (set by ide_read_sectors).
   161 0000125B 2EC606[3B11]00      <1>     mov byte [cs:int13_last_status], 0
   162 00001261 30E4                <1>     xor ah, ah
   163 00001263 F8                  <1>     clc
   164 00001264 CF                  <1>     iret
   165                              <1> 
   166                              <1> .chs_error_pop:
   167 00001265 07                  <1>     pop es
   168 00001266 665F                <1>     pop edi
   169 00001268 665A                <1>     pop edx
   170 0000126A 6659                <1>     pop ecx
   171 0000126C 665B                <1>     pop ebx
   172 0000126E 6658                <1>     pop eax
   173                              <1> .chs_error:
   174 00001270 2EC606[3B11]01      <1>     mov byte [cs:int13_last_status], 0x01
   175 00001276 B401                <1>     mov ah, 0x01
   176 00001278 F9                  <1>     stc
   177 00001279 CF                  <1>     iret
   178                              <1> 
   179                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   180                              <1> ; AH=08h: Get drive parameters.
   181                              <1> ;   DL = drive number.
   182                              <1> ;   Returns: CH = max cyl low, CL[7:6] = max cyl high, CL[5:0] = max sector,
   183                              <1> ;            DH = max head, DL = number of drives.
   184                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   185                              <1> .get_drive_params:
   186 0000127A 80FA80              <1>     cmp dl, 0x80
   187 0000127D 7408                <1>     je .gdp_hdd
   188 0000127F 80FAE0              <1>     cmp dl, 0xE0
   189 00001282 7436                <1>     je .gdp_cd
   190 00001284 E98000              <1>     jmp .gdp_no_drive
   191                              <1> 
   192                              <1> .gdp_hdd:
   193 00001287 2E803E[BE09]00      <1>     cmp byte [cs:ide_master_present], 0
   194 0000128D 7478                <1>     je .gdp_no_drive
   195                              <1> 
   196 0000128F 53                  <1>     push bx
   197 00001290 2EA1[BF09]          <1>     mov ax, [cs:ide_master_cyls]
   198 00001294 48                  <1>     dec ax                      ; Max cylinder (0-based)
   199 00001295 88C5                <1>     mov ch, al                  ; Low 8 bits of cylinder
   200 00001297 88E1                <1>     mov cl, ah
   201 00001299 C0E106              <1>     shl cl, 6                   ; High 2 bits into CL[7:6]
   202                              <1> 
   203 0000129C 2EA1[C309]          <1>     mov ax, [cs:ide_master_spt]
   204 000012A0 243F                <1>     and al, 0x3F
   205 000012A2 08C1                <1>     or cl, al                   ; Max sector in CL[5:0]
   206                              <1> 
   207 000012A4 2EA1[C109]          <1>     mov ax, [cs:ide_master_heads]
   208 000012A8 48                  <1>     dec ax
   209 000012A9 88C6                <1>     mov dh, al                  ; Max head (0-based)
   210                              <1> 
   211 000012AB B201                <1>     mov dl, 1                   ; 1 drive
   212 000012AD 2EC606[3B11]00      <1>     mov byte [cs:int13_last_status], 0
   213 000012B3 31C0                <1>     xor ax, ax                  ; AH = 0 (success)
   214 000012B5 B300                <1>     mov bl, 0                   ; Drive type (not applicable for HD)
   215 000012B7 5B                  <1>     pop bx
   216 000012B8 F8                  <1>     clc
   217 000012B9 CF                  <1>     iret
   218                              <1> 
   219                              <1> .gdp_cd:
   220 000012BA 2E803E[BE09]00      <1>     cmp byte [cs:ide_master_present], 0
   221 000012C0 7445                <1>     je .gdp_no_drive
   222                              <1> 
   223                              <1>     ; Synthetic CHS for CD no-emulation compatibility.
   224                              <1>     ; Heads = 64, SPT = 32, cylinders derived from total sectors.
   225 000012C2 53                  <1>     push bx
   226 000012C3 2E66A1[C509]        <1>     mov eax, [cs:ide_master_lba28]
   227 000012C8 6631D2              <1>     xor edx, edx
   228 000012CB 66BB00080000        <1>     mov ebx, 2048               ; 64 * 32
   229 000012D1 66F7F3              <1>     div ebx                     ; EAX = cylinders
   230 000012D4 6685C0              <1>     test eax, eax
   231 000012D7 7402                <1>     jz .gdp_cd_cyl_zero
   232 000012D9 6648                <1>     dec eax
   233                              <1> .gdp_cd_cyl_zero:
   234 000012DB 663DFF030000        <1>     cmp eax, 1023
   235 000012E1 7606                <1>     jbe .gdp_cd_cyl_ok
   236 000012E3 66B8FF030000        <1>     mov eax, 1023
   237                              <1> .gdp_cd_cyl_ok:
   238                              <1> 
   239 000012E9 88C5                <1>     mov ch, al                  ; Cylinder low 8 bits
   240 000012EB 88E1                <1>     mov cl, ah                  ; Cylinder high 2 bits in AH[1:0]
   241 000012ED 80E103              <1>     and cl, 0x03
   242 000012F0 C0E106              <1>     shl cl, 6
   243 000012F3 80C920              <1>     or cl, 32                   ; SPT = 32
   244                              <1> 
   245 000012F6 B63F                <1>     mov dh, 63                  ; Heads = 64 => max head 63
   246 000012F8 B201                <1>     mov dl, 1                   ; 1 drive
   247 000012FA 2EC606[3B11]00      <1>     mov byte [cs:int13_last_status], 0

   257 0000130F B200                <1>     mov dl, 0

   261                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   262                              <1> ; AH=15h: Get disk type.
   263                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   264                              <1> .get_disk_type:
   265 00001313 80FA80              <1>     cmp dl, 0x80
   266 00001316 7521                <1>     jne .gdt_none

   268 0000131E 7419                <1>     je .gdt_none
   269                              <1> 
   270 00001320 B403                <1>     mov ah, 0x03                ; Type 3 = hard disk
   271                              <1>     ; CX:DX = total sectors.
   272 00001322 2E66A1[C509]        <1>     mov eax, [cs:ide_master_lba28]
   273 00001327 89C1                <1>     mov cx, ax
   274 00001329 66C1E810            <1>     shr eax, 16
   275 0000132D 89C2                <1>     mov dx, ax
   276 0000132F 87CA                <1>     xchg cx, dx                 ; CX = high, DX = low
   277 00001331 2EC606[3B11]00      <1>     mov byte [cs:int13_last_status], 0
   278 00001337 F8                  <1>     clc
   279 00001338 CF                  <1>     iret

   283 0000133F B400                <1>     mov ah, 0x00                ; No drive
   284 00001341 F8                  <1>     clc
   285 00001342 CF                  <1>     iret

   287                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   288                              <1> ; AH=41h: Check INT 13h extensions.
   289                              <1> ;   BX = 0x55AA, DL = drive.
   290                              <1> ;   Returns: BX = 0xAA55, AH = version, CX = API bitmap.
   291                              <1> ;
   292                              <1> ;   Supported drives: 0x80 (HDD) and 0xE0 (virtual CD-ROM, El Torito).
   293                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   294                              <1> .check_extensions:
   295                              <1>     ; Accept 0x80 (HDD) and 0xE0 (virtual CD) only.
   296 00001343 80FA80              <1>     cmp dl, 0x80

   300 0000134D EB16                <1>     jmp .chk_ext_fail
   301                              <1> 
   302                              <1> .chk_ext_ok:
   303 0000134F 81FBAA55            <1>     cmp bx, 0x55AA
   304 00001353 7510                <1>     jne .chk_ext_fail
   305                              <1> 
   306 00001355 BB55AA              <1>     mov bx, 0xAA55
   307 00001358 B430                <1>     mov ah, 0x30                ; Version 3.0
   308 0000135A B90100              <1>     mov cx, 0x0001              ; Extended disk access supported
   309 0000135D 2EC606[3B11]00      <1>     mov byte [cs:int13_last_status], 0

   319                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   320                              <1> ; AH=42h: Extended read sectors (LBA).
   321                              <1> ;   DL = drive, DS:SI = Disk Address Packet (DAP).
   322                              <1> ;
   323                              <1> ;   DAP format:
   324                              <1> ;     Byte 0: size (16)
   325                              <1> ;     Byte 1: reserved (0)
   326                              <1> ;     Word 2: sector count
   327                              <1> ;     Word 4: buffer offset
   328                              <1> ;     Word 6: buffer segment
   329                              <1> ;     Qword 8: starting LBA
   330                              <1> ;
   331                              <1> ;   For DL=0xE0 (virtual CD-ROM): use standard EDD DAP semantics
   332                              <1> ;   (LBA + count in 512-byte sectors), same as HDD.  Linux El Torito
   333                              <1> ;   bootloaders (e.g. ISOLINUX) already convert catalog RBAs to 512-byte
   334                              <1> ;   units before calling AH=42.
   335                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   336                              <1> .extended_read:
   337 0000136F 6650                <1>     push eax
   338 00001371 6653                <1>     push ebx
   339 00001373 6651                <1>     push ecx
   340 00001375 6652                <1>     push edx
   341 00001377 6657                <1>     push edi
   342 00001379 06                  <1>     push es
   343 0000137A 1E                  <1>     push ds
   344 0000137B 56                  <1>     push si
   345                              <1> 
   346                              <1>     ; BIOS variables use cs: prefix (in the ROM segment) so they are safe
   347                              <1>     ; regardless of caller's DS.  Save caller's DS in BX for DAP access.
   348 0000137C 8CDB                <1>     mov bx, ds
   349                              <1> 
   350 0000137E 2E803E[BE09]00      <1>     cmp byte [cs:ide_master_present], 0

   352                              <1>

   358                              <1>

   360                              <1>     ; Read DAP fields via caller's DS (saved in BX), using ES as proxy segment.
   361 00001392 53                  <1>     push bx

   363 00001394 26660FB74C02        <1>     movzx ecx, word [es:si + 2]    ; Sector count (512-byte sectors)
   364 0000139A 268B7C04            <1>     mov di, [es:si + 4]            ; Buffer offset
   365 0000139E 268B5C06            <1>     mov bx, [es:si + 6]            ; Buffer segment
   366 000013A2 26668B4408          <1>     mov eax, [es:si + 8]           ; LBA (512-byte sector units)
   367 000013A7 8EC3                <1>     mov es, bx                      ; ES = buffer segment
   368 000013A9 E86D02              <1>     call ide_read_sectors
   369 000013AC 7235                <1>     jc .ext_read_fail_pop

   371                              <1>

   376 000013B2 26660FB74C02        <1>     movzx ecx, word [es:si + 2]    ; Sector count (512-byte sectors)
   377 000013B8 268B7C04            <1>     mov di, [es:si + 4]            ; Buffer offset

   379 000013C0 26668B4408          <1>     mov eax, [es:si + 8]           ; LBA (512-byte sector units)
   380 000013C5 8EC3                <1>     mov es, bx                      ; ES = buffer segment

   387 000013CE 07                  <1>     pop es
   388 000013CF 665F                <1>     pop edi
   389 000013D1 665A                <1>     pop edx
   390 000013D3 6659                <1>     pop ecx

   471                              <1> ; AH=48h: Get extended drive parameters.
   472                              <1> ;   DL = drive, DS:SI = result buffer.
   473                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   474                              <1> .get_ext_params:
   475 00001458 2E803E[BE09]00      <1>     cmp byte [cs:ide_master_present], 0
   476 0000145E 0F849C00            <1>     je .gep_fail
   477                              <1> 
   478 00001462 80FA80              <1>     cmp dl, 0x80
   479 00001465 7408                <1>     je .gep_hdd
   480 00001467 80FAE0              <1>     cmp dl, 0xE0
   481 0000146A 744D                <1>     je .gep_cd
   482 0000146C E98F00              <1>     jmp .gep_fail
   483                              <1> 
   484                              <1> .gep_hdd:
   485                              <1>     ; Buffer size (minimum 26 bytes).
   486 0000146F C7041A00            <1>     mov word [si + 0], 26       ; Size of result
   487 00001473 C744020200          <1>     mov word [si + 2], 0x0002   ; Flags: CHS valid
   488                              <1>

   490 00001478 2E660FB706[BF09]    <1>     movzx eax, word [cs:ide_master_cyls]
   491 0000147F 66894404            <1>     mov [si + 4], eax           ; Cylinders
   492 00001483 2E660FB706[C109]    <1>     movzx eax, word [cs:ide_master_heads]
   493 0000148A 66894408            <1>     mov [si + 8], eax           ; Heads
   494 0000148E 2E660FB706[C309]    <1>     movzx eax, word [cs:ide_master_spt]
   495 00001495 6689440C            <1>     mov [si + 12], eax          ; Sectors per track
   496                              <1> 
   497                              <1>     ; Total sectors (64-bit, in 512-byte units).
   498 00001499 2E66A1[C509]        <1>     mov eax, [cs:ide_master_lba28]
   499 0000149E 66894410            <1>     mov [si + 16], eax
   500 000014A2 66C7441400000000    <1>     mov dword [si + 20], 0      ; High dword
   501                              <1>

   504                              <1> 
   505 000014AF 2EC606[3B11]00      <1>     mov byte [cs:int13_last_status], 0
   506 000014B5 30E4                <1>     xor ah, ah
   507 000014B7 F8                  <1>     clc
   508 000014B8 CF                  <1>     iret

   510                              <1> .gep_cd:
   511                              <1>     ; Virtual CD-ROM: report 2048-byte sectors.
   512                              <1>     ; Total capacity in 2048-byte sectors = total_ata_sectors / 4.
   513 000014B9 C7041A00            <1>     mov word [si + 0], 26       ; Size of result
   514 000014BD C744020000          <1>     mov word [si + 2], 0x0000   ; Flags: no CHS (CD-ROM)
   515 000014C2 66C7440400000000    <1>     mov dword [si + 4],  0      ; Cylinders (N/A)
   516 000014CA 66C7440800000000    <1>     mov dword [si + 8],  0      ; Heads (N/A)
   517 000014D2 66C7440C00000000    <1>     mov dword [si + 12], 0      ; Sectors per track (N/A)
   518                              <1> 
   519                              <1>     ; Total ISO sectors = ATA sectors / 4.
   520 000014DA 2E66A1[C509]        <1>     mov eax, [cs:ide_master_lba28]
   521 000014DF 66C1E802            <1>     shr eax, 2
   522 000014E3 66894410            <1>     mov [si + 16], eax
   523 000014E7 66C7441400000000    <1>     mov dword [si + 20], 0      ; High dword
   524                              <1> 
   525                              <1>     ; Bytes per sector: 2048.
   526 000014EF C744180008          <1>     mov word [si + 24], 2048
   527                              <1> 
   528 000014F4 2EC606[3B11]00      <1>     mov byte [cs:int13_last_status], 0
   529 000014FA 30E4                <1>     xor ah, ah
   530 000014FC F8                  <1>     clc
   531 000014FD CF                  <1>     iret
   532                              <1> 
   533                              <1> .gep_fail:
   534 000014FE 2EC606[3B11]01      <1>     mov byte [cs:int13_last_status], 0x01
   535 00001504 B401                <1>     mov ah, 0x01
   536 00001506 F9                  <1>     stc
   537 00001507 CF                  <1>     iret
   538                              <1> 
   539                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   540                              <1> ; AH=4Bh: Get/Terminate Disk Emulation Status (El Torito).
   541                              <1> ;   AL = 0x00 → Terminate disk emulation (we treat as status query too)
   542                              <1> ;   AL = 0x01 → Return Emulation Status
   543                              <1> ;   DL = drive number
   544                              <1> ;
   545                              <1> ; Fills the 20-byte El Torito Specification Packet at DS:SI:
   546                              <1> ;   +0   (byte)  packet size = 0x13
   547                              <1> ;   +1   (byte)  boot media type
   548                              <1> ;   +2   (byte)  drive number of the emulated drive
   549                              <1> ;   +3   (byte)  controller index (0)
   550                              <1> ;   +4   (dword) image start (Load RBA in CD-sector units)
   551                              <1> ;   +8   (word)  device specification (0)
   552                              <1> ;   +10  (word)  user buffer segment (0)
   553                              <1> ;   +12  (word)  load segment
   554                              <1> ;   +14  (word)  sector count (512-byte virtual sectors)
   555                              <1> ;   +16  (word)  CHS of emulated drive (0 for no-emulation)
   556                              <1> ;   +18  (byte)  reserved (0)
   557                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   558                              <1> .get_cd_emul_status:
   559                              <1>         ; Only support subfunctions 00h (terminate/query) and 01h (status).
   560 00001508 3C00                <1>         cmp al, 0x00
   561 0000150A 7406                <1>         je .gcds_sub_ok
   562 0000150C 3C01                <1>         cmp al, 0x01
   563 0000150E 7402                <1>         je .gcds_sub_ok
   564 00001510 EB6A                <1>         jmp .gcds_fail          ; Nothing pushed yet — skip pops
   565                              <1>     .gcds_sub_ok:
   566                              <1> 
   567                              <1>     ; Save caller's DS in ES for output-buffer writes (ES:SI).
   568 00001512 06                  <1>     push es
   569 00001513 8CD8                <1>     mov ax, ds
   570 00001515 8EC0                <1>     mov es, ax          ; ES = caller's DS
   571                              <1> 
   572                              <1>     ; All el_torito_* variables are in the BIOS ROM segment (cs:).
   573                              <1>     ; No DS change needed — use cs: prefix for every BIOS variable read.
   574                              <1> 
   575                              <1>     ; Caller's DL must match our El Torito drive.
   576 00001517 2E3A16[1019]        <1>     cmp dl, [cs:el_torito_drive]

   586 00001532 2EA0[1019]          <1>     mov al, [cs:el_torito_drive]
   587 00001536 26884402            <1>     mov [es:si + 2], al            ; Drive number
   588 0000153A 26C6440300          <1>     mov byte [es:si + 3],  0x00    ; Controller index

   720                              <1> ; ide_read_sectors — Read sectors from IDE master using PIO LBA28.

   799                              <1>

   808 00001695 F36D                <1>     rep insw
   809 00001697 5A                  <1>     pop dx

   813 0000169B 6649                <1>     dec ecx                     ; Decrement count

    26                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    27                              <1> ; AX=E820h: Get system memory map.
    28                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    29                              <1> i15_e820:
    30 00001796 6681FA50414D53      <1>     cmp edx, 0x534D4150
    31 0000179D 7552                <1>     jne .fail
    32                              <1> 
    33 0000179F 6656                <1>     push esi
    34 000017A1 1E                  <1>     push ds
    35                              <1> 
    36                              <1>     ; The E820 table lives in the BIOS ROM segment (cs:).
    37                              <1>     ; memory_detect wrote it with DS=CS; we read it with DS=CS too.
    38 000017A2 0E                  <1>     push cs
    39 000017A3 1F                  <1>     pop ds
    40                              <1> 
    41 000017A4 660FB706[CB03]      <1>     movzx eax, word [e820_count]
    42 000017AA 6639C3              <1>     cmp ebx, eax
    43 000017AD 7D3F                <1>     jge .e820_fail_pop
    44                              <1> 
    45 000017AF 6689D8              <1>     mov eax, ebx
    46 000017B2 66BE18000000        <1>     mov esi, E820_ENTRY_SIZE
    47 000017B8 66F7E6              <1>     mul esi
    48 000017BB 6605[CD030000]      <1>     add eax, e820_table         ; ESI = &e820_table[entry_index]
    49 000017C1 6689C6              <1>     mov esi, eax
    50                              <1> 
    51 000017C4 51                  <1>     push cx
    52 000017C5 B91400              <1>     mov cx, 20
    53 000017C8 57                  <1>     push di
    54 000017C9 F3A4                <1>     rep movsb                   ; DS:SI (segment 0) → ES:DI
    55 000017CB 5F                  <1>     pop di
    56 000017CC 59                  <1>     pop cx
    57                              <1> 
    58 000017CD 66B850414D53        <1>     mov eax, 0x534D4150
    59 000017D3 6643                <1>     inc ebx
    60 000017D5 660FB70E[CB03]      <1>     movzx ecx, word [e820_count]
    61 000017DB 6639CB              <1>     cmp ebx, ecx
    62 000017DE 7C03                <1>     jl .e820_not_last
    63 000017E0 6631DB              <1>     xor ebx, ebx
    64                              <1> .e820_not_last:
    65 000017E3 66B914000000        <1>     mov ecx, 20
    66                              <1> 
    67 000017E9 1F                  <1>     pop ds
    68 000017EA 665E                <1>     pop esi
    69 000017EC F8                  <1>     clc
    70 000017ED CF                  <1>     iret
    71                              <1> 
    72                              <1> .e820_fail_pop:
    73 000017EE 1F                  <1>     pop ds
    74 000017EF 665E                <1>     pop esi
    75                              <1> .fail:
    76 000017F1 B486                <1>     mov ah, 0x86
    77 000017F3 F9                  <1>     stc
    78 000017F4 CF                  <1>     iret
    79                              <1> 
    80                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    81                              <1> ; AH=88h: Get extended memory size.

    84 000017F5 6653                <1>     push ebx
    85 000017F7 2E66A1[8D04]        <1>     mov eax, [cs:ram_size_bytes]
    86 000017FC 662D00001000        <1>     sub eax, 0x100000
    87 00001802 66C1E80A            <1>     shr eax, 10
    88 00001806 663DFFFF0000        <1>     cmp eax, 0xFFFF
    89 0000180C 7606                <1>     jbe .ok
    90 0000180E 66B8FFFF0000        <1>     mov eax, 0xFFFF
    91                              <1> .ok:
    92 00001814 665B                <1>     pop ebx
    93 00001816 F8                  <1>     clc
    94 00001817 CF                  <1>     iret

    99                              <1> i15_e801:
   100 00001818 6656                <1>     push esi
   101 0000181A 2E66A1[8D04]        <1>     mov eax, [cs:ram_size_bytes]
   102                              <1> 
   103 0000181F 663D00000001        <1>     cmp eax, 0x1000000
   104 00001825 7611                <1>     jbe .below_16
   105                              <1> 
   106 00001827 B9003C              <1>     mov cx, 15360
   107 0000182A 89C8                <1>     mov ax, cx
   108 0000182C 662D00000001        <1>     sub eax, 0x1000000
   109 00001832 66C1E810            <1>     shr eax, 16
   110 00001836 EB12                <1>     jmp .set
   111                              <1>

   114 0000183E 66C1E80A            <1>     shr eax, 10
   115 00001842 89C1                <1>     mov cx, ax
   116 00001844 31DB                <1>     xor bx, bx
   117 00001846 31D2                <1>     xor dx, dx
   118 00001848 EB06                <1>     jmp .done
   119                              <1>

   121 0000184A 89C3                <1>     mov bx, ax
   122 0000184C 89C2                <1>     mov dx, ax
   123 0000184E 89C8                <1>     mov ax, cx
   124                              <1> 
   125                              <1> .done:
   126 00001850 665E                <1>     pop esi
   127 00001852 F8                  <1>     clc
   128 00001853 CF                  <1>     iret
   129                              <1> 
   130                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   131                              <1> ; AH=C0h: Get system configuration table pointer.

   133                              <1> i15_get_config_table:
   134 00001854 BB[6218]            <1>     mov bx, i15_sys_config_table

   136 00001858 B800F0              <1>     mov ax, 0xF000
   137 0000185B 8EC0                <1>     mov es, ax
   138 0000185D 58                  <1>     pop ax
   139 0000185E 30E4                <1>     xor ah, ah
   140 00001860 F8                  <1>     clc
   141 00001861 CF                  <1>     iret

   144 00001862 0800                <1>     dw 8                        ; Table length

   146 00001865 01                  <1>     db 0x01                     ; Sub-model
   147 00001866 00                  <1>     db 0x00                     ; BIOS revision
   148 00001867 74                  <1>     db 0x74                     ; Feature byte 1
   149 00001868 00000000            <1>     db 0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00  ; Feature bytes 2-5

     1                              <1> ; =============================================================================
     2                              <1> ; int16h.asm — INT 16h: Keyboard BIOS services
     3                              <1> ; =============================================================================
