     5                              <1> int16h_handler:
     6 0000187D 80FC00              <1>     cmp ah, 0x00
     7 00001880 741A                <1>     je .wait_key
     8 00001882 80FC01              <1>     cmp ah, 0x01
     9 00001885 7443                <1>     je .check_key
    10 00001887 80FC02              <1>     cmp ah, 0x02
    11 0000188A 746B                <1>     je .get_shift_flags
    12 0000188C 80FC10              <1>     cmp ah, 0x10
    13 0000188F 740B                <1>     je .wait_key              ; Enhanced: same as 00h for us
    14 00001891 80FC11              <1>     cmp ah, 0x11
    15 00001894 7434                <1>     je .check_key             ; Enhanced: same as 01h
    16 00001896 80FC12              <1>     cmp ah, 0x12
    17 00001899 7466                <1>     je .get_ext_shift_flags
    18                              <1>     ; Unsupported.
    19 0000189B CF                  <1>     iret
    20                              <1> 
    21                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    22                              <1> ; AH=00h/10h: Wait for keypress.
    23                              <1> ;   Returns: AH = scan code, AL = ASCII code.
    24                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    25                              <1> .wait_key:
    26 0000189C 1E                  <1>     push ds
    27 0000189D 53                  <1>     push bx
    28 0000189E 31DB                <1>     xor bx, bx
    29 000018A0 8EDB                <1>     mov ds, bx
    30                              <1> 
    31                              <1> .wait_loop:
    32 000018A2 FA                  <1>     cli
    33 000018A3 8B1E1A04            <1>     mov bx, [BDA_KBD_BUF_HEAD]
    34 000018A7 3B1E1C04            <1>     cmp bx, [BDA_KBD_BUF_TAIL]
    35 000018AB 7504                <1>     jne .key_available
    36 000018AD FB                  <1>     sti
    37 000018AE F4                  <1>     hlt                         ; Wait for keyboard IRQ
    38 000018AF EBF1                <1>     jmp .wait_loop
    39                              <1> 
    40                              <1> .key_available:
    41                              <1>     ; Read key from buffer.
    42 000018B1 8B870004            <1>     mov ax, [bx + 0x0400]      ; BDA buffer is at 0x400 + offset
    43                              <1>     ; Advance head pointer.
    44 000018B5 83C302              <1>     add bx, 2
    45 000018B8 3B1E8204            <1>     cmp bx, [BDA_KBD_BUF_END]
    46 000018BC 7204                <1>     jb .head_ok
    47 000018BE 8B1E8004            <1>     mov bx, [BDA_KBD_BUF_START]
    48                              <1> .head_ok:
    49 000018C2 891E1A04            <1>     mov [BDA_KBD_BUF_HEAD], bx
    50 000018C6 FB                  <1>     sti
    51                              <1> 
    52 000018C7 5B                  <1>     pop bx
    53 000018C8 1F                  <1>     pop ds
    54 000018C9 CF                  <1>     iret
    55                              <1> 
    56                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    57                              <1> ; AH=01h/11h: Check keyboard buffer (non-blocking).
    58                              <1> ;   Returns: ZF=1 if empty, ZF=0 if key available (AX = key, not removed).
    59                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    60                              <1> .check_key:
    61 000018CA 1E                  <1>     push ds
    62 000018CB 53                  <1>     push bx
    63 000018CC 31DB                <1>     xor bx, bx
    64 000018CE 8EDB                <1>     mov ds, bx
    65                              <1> 
    66 000018D0 FA                  <1>     cli
    67 000018D1 8B1E1A04            <1>     mov bx, [BDA_KBD_BUF_HEAD]
    68 000018D5 3B1E1C04            <1>     cmp bx, [BDA_KBD_BUF_TAIL]
    69 000018D9 7410                <1>     je .buf_empty
    70                              <1> 
    71                              <1>     ; Key available: peek without removing.
    72 000018DB 8B870004            <1>     mov ax, [bx + 0x0400]
    73 000018DF FB                  <1>     sti
    74                              <1> 
    75 000018E0 5B                  <1>     pop bx
    76 000018E1 1F                  <1>     pop ds
    77                              <1>     ; Clear ZF to indicate key available.
    78 000018E2 55                  <1>     push bp
    79 000018E3 89E5                <1>     mov bp, sp
    80 000018E5 836606BF            <1>     and word [bp + 6], ~0x0040  ; Clear ZF in saved FLAGS on stack
    81 000018E9 5D                  <1>     pop bp
    82 000018EA CF                  <1>     iret
    83                              <1> 
    84                              <1> .buf_empty:
    85 000018EB FB                  <1>     sti
    86 000018EC 5B                  <1>     pop bx
    87 000018ED 1F                  <1>     pop ds
    88                              <1>     ; Set ZF to indicate empty.
    89 000018EE 55                  <1>     push bp
    90 000018EF 89E5                <1>     mov bp, sp
    91 000018F1 834E0640            <1>     or word [bp + 6], 0x0040    ; Set ZF in saved FLAGS on stack
    92 000018F5 5D                  <1>     pop bp
    93 000018F6 CF                  <1>     iret
    94                              <1> 
    95                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    96                              <1> ; AH=02h: Get shift flags.
    97                              <1> ;   Returns: AL = shift flags byte 1.
    98                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    99                              <1> .get_shift_flags:
   100 000018F7 1E                  <1>     push ds
   101 000018F8 31C0                <1>     xor ax, ax
   102 000018FA 8ED8                <1>     mov ds, ax
   103 000018FC A01704              <1>     mov al, [BDA_KBD_FLAGS1]
   104 000018FF 1F                  <1>     pop ds
   105 00001900 CF                  <1>     iret
   106                              <1> 
   107                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   108                              <1> ; AH=12h: Get extended shift flags.
   109                              <1> ;   Returns: AL = flags byte 1, AH = flags byte 2.
   110                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   111                              <1> .get_ext_shift_flags:
   112 00001901 1E                  <1>     push ds
   113 00001902 31C0                <1>     xor ax, ax
   114 00001904 8ED8                <1>     mov ds, ax
   115 00001906 A01704              <1>     mov al, [BDA_KBD_FLAGS1]
   116 00001909 8A261804            <1>     mov ah, [BDA_KBD_FLAGS2]
   117 0000190D 1F                  <1>     pop ds
   118 0000190E CF                  <1>     iret
    29                                  %include "int19h.asm"
     1                              <1> ; =============================================================================
     2                              <1> ; int19h.asm — INT 19h: Bootstrap loader
     3                              <1> ;
     4                              <1> ; Boot priority:
     5                              <1> ;   1. El Torito CD boot (reads ISO 9660 Boot Record VD at ISO sector 17 =
     6                              <1> ;      ATA LBA 68, parses the Boot Catalog, loads the boot image).
     7                              <1> ;   2. MBR hard-disk boot (reads ATA LBA 0, checks 0xAA55 signature).
     8                              <1> ;
     9                              <1> ; ISO ↔ ATA sector translation:
    10                              <1> ;   ISO uses 2048-byte logical sectors; the ATA device uses 512-byte sectors.
    11                              <1> ;   ATA LBA = ISO sector × 4.
    12                              <1> ;
    13                              <1> ; After a successful El Torito boot the following variables are filled and
    14                              <1> ; can be queried by INT 13h AH=4Bh:
    15                              <1> ;   el_torito_drive     — virtual drive number handed to the boot image in DL
    16                              <1> ;   el_torito_emul      — Boot Media Type byte from the catalog entry
    17                              <1> ;   el_torito_load_seg  — segment the boot image was loaded into
    18                              <1> ;   el_torito_rba       — Load RBA from the catalog (ISO 2048-byte sector units)
    19                              <1> ;   el_torito_count     — virtual sector count that was loaded
    20                              <1> ; =============================================================================
    21                              <1> 
    22                              <1> ; ── El Torito runtime data (queried by INT 13h AH=4Bh) ─────────────────────
    23 0000190F 00                  <1> el_torito_present:  db 0       ; 1 after successful El Torito detection
    24 00001910 E0                  <1> el_torito_drive:    db 0xE0    ; Virtual drive number returned in DL (0xE0=CD)
    25 00001911 00                  <1> el_torito_emul:     db 0       ; Boot Media Type (0=no-emul, 1-3=floppy, 4=HD)
    26 00001912 C007                <1> el_torito_load_seg: dw 0x07C0  ; Load segment (from catalog or default 0x07C0)
    27 00001914 00000000            <1> el_torito_rba:      dd 0       ; Boot image Load RBA (ISO 2048-byte sectors)
    28 00001918 0100                <1> el_torito_count:    dw 1       ; Sector count (512-byte virtual sectors loaded)
    29 0000191A 00                  <1> el_torito_handoff:  db 0       ; Set to 1 right before jumping into boot image
    30                              <1> 
    31                              <1> ; ── Work buffer ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    32                              <1> ; ide_identify_buf is only needed during POST (ide_detect). After POST it is
    33                              <1> ; safe to reuse those 512 bytes as a scratch buffer for El Torito parsing.
    34                              <1> el_torito_buf       equ ide_identify_buf
    35                              <1> 
    36                              <1> ; ── INT 19h entry ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    37                              <1> int19h_handler:
    38 0000191B FB                  <1>     sti
    39                              <1> 
    40 0000191C 2E803E[BE09]00      <1>     cmp byte [cs:ide_master_present], 0
    41 00001922 7438                <1>     je .no_boot
    42                              <1> 
    43                              <1>     ; ── Step 1: Try El Torito ──────────────────────────────────────────────
    44 00001924 E83F00              <1>     call detect_el_torito
    45 00001927 7205                <1>     jc .try_hd             ; CF=1 → not an El Torito disk
    46                              <1> 
    47                              <1>     ; El Torito signature verified — load and jump.
    48 00001929 E84301              <1>     call boot_el_torito
    49                              <1>     ; boot_el_torito only returns on failure.
    50 0000192C EB2E                <1>     jmp .no_boot
    51                              <1> 
    52                              <1>     ; ── Step 2: Fall back to MBR/HDD boot ─────────────────────────────────
    53                              <1> .try_hd:
    54 0000192E BE[0301]            <1>     mov si, str_booting_hd
    55 00001931 E8C309              <1>     call bios_print
    56                              <1> 
    57                              <1>     ; Read LBA 0 (MBR) to 0x0000:0x7C00.
    58 00001934 66B800000000        <1>     mov eax, 0
    59 0000193A 66B901000000        <1>     mov ecx, 1
    60 00001940 06                  <1>     push es
    61 00001941 6A00                <1>     push word 0x0000
    62 00001943 07                  <1>     pop es
    63 00001944 BF007C              <1>     mov di, 0x7C00
    64 00001947 E8CFFC              <1>     call ide_read_sectors
    65 0000194A 07                  <1>     pop es
    66 0000194B 720F                <1>     jc .no_boot
    67                              <1> 
    68                              <1>     ; Verify MBR signature.
    69 0000194D 813EFE7D55AA        <1>     cmp word [0x7C00 + 510], 0xAA55
    70 00001953 7507                <1>     jne .no_boot
    71                              <1> 
    72                              <1>     ; Valid MBR — launch it.
    73 00001955 B280                <1>     mov dl, 0x80            ; First HDD
    74 00001957 EA007C0000          <1>     jmp 0x0000:0x7C00
    75                              <1> 
    76                              <1> .no_boot:
    77 0000195C BE[3801]            <1>     mov si, str_no_boot
    78 0000195F E89509              <1>     call bios_print
    79                              <1> .halt:
    80 00001962 FA                  <1>     cli
    81 00001963 F4                  <1>     hlt
    82 00001964 EBFC                <1>     jmp .halt
    83                              <1> 
    84                              <1> 
    85                              <1> ; =============================================================================
    86                              <1> ; detect_el_torito
    87                              <1> ;
    88                              <1> ; Checks whether the attached IDE disk is an El Torito CD image.
    89                              <1> ; Reads ISO sector 17 (ATA LBA 68) which, for a standards-compliant ISO,
    90                              <1> ; contains the Boot Record Volume Descriptor.  On success it reads the Boot
    91                              <1> ; Catalog and fills all el_torito_* variables.
    92                              <1> ;
    93                              <1> ; Returns: CF=0 on success, CF=1 if this is not an El Torito disk.
    94                              <1> ; Clobbers: el_torito_buf (= ide_identify_buf)
    95                              <1> ; =============================================================================
    96                              <1> detect_el_torito:
    97 00001966 6650                <1>     push eax
    98 00001968 6651                <1>     push ecx
    99 0000196A 6657                <1>     push edi
   100 0000196C 06                  <1>     push es
   101                              <1> 
   102                              <1>     ; ── Read ISO sector 17 = ATA LBA 68 ──────────────────────────────────
   103                              <1>     ; The Boot Record Volume Descriptor occupies bytes 0–2047 of ISO sector 17.
   104                              <1>     ; We only need the first 512 bytes (fits in one ATA sector).
   105 0000196D 66B844000000        <1>     mov eax, 68             ; ATA LBA 68 = ISO sector 17 (17 × 4 = 68)
   106 00001973 66B901000000        <1>     mov ecx, 1
   107 00001979 1E                  <1>     push ds
   108 0000197A 07                  <1>     pop es
   109 0000197B BF[D209]            <1>     mov di, el_torito_buf
   110 0000197E E898FC              <1>     call ide_read_sectors
   111 00001981 0F82E100            <1>     jc .fail
   112                              <1> 
   113                              <1>     ; ── Validate Volume Descriptor type = 0 (Boot Record) ─────────────────
   114 00001985 803E[D209]00        <1>     cmp byte [el_torito_buf + 0], 0x00
   115 0000198A 0F85D800            <1>     jne .fail
   116                              <1> 
   117                              <1>     ; ── Validate Standard Identifier "CD001" at bytes 1–5 ─────────────────
   118 0000198E 803E[D309]43        <1>     cmp byte [el_torito_buf + 1], 'C'
   119 00001993 0F85CF00            <1>     jne .fail
   120 00001997 803E[D409]44        <1>     cmp byte [el_torito_buf + 2], 'D'
   121 0000199C 0F85C600            <1>     jne .fail
   122 000019A0 803E[D509]30        <1>     cmp byte [el_torito_buf + 3], '0'
   123 000019A5 0F85BD00            <1>     jne .fail
   124 000019A9 803E[D609]30        <1>     cmp byte [el_torito_buf + 4], '0'
   125 000019AE 0F85B400            <1>     jne .fail
   126 000019B2 803E[D709]31        <1>     cmp byte [el_torito_buf + 5], '1'
   127 000019B7 0F85AB00            <1>     jne .fail
   128                              <1> 
   129                              <1>     ; ── Validate Boot System Identifier = "EL TORITO SPECIFICATION" ───────
   130                              <1>     ; Bytes 7–38.  We check the first 8 characters ("EL TORIT") for speed.
   131 000019BB 66813E[D909]454C20- <1>     cmp dword [el_torito_buf + 7],  'EL T'
   131 000019C3 54                  <1>
   132 000019C4 0F859E00            <1>     jne .fail
   133 000019C8 66813E[DD09]4F5249- <1>     cmp dword [el_torito_buf + 11], 'ORIT'
   133 000019D0 54                  <1>
   134 000019D1 0F859100            <1>     jne .fail
   135                              <1> 
   136                              <1>     ; ── Read Boot Catalog ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
   137                              <1>     ; Boot Catalog location in ISO sector units is at bytes 71–74 (DWORD LE).
   138                              <1>     ; Convert to ATA LBA by multiplying by 4.
   139 000019D5 66A1[190A]          <1>     mov eax, [el_torito_buf + 71]   ; ISO sector of Boot Catalog
   140 000019D9 66C1E002            <1>     shl eax, 2                       ; × 4 → ATA LBA
   141 000019DD 66B901000000        <1>     mov ecx, 1
   142 000019E3 1E                  <1>     push ds
   143 000019E4 07                  <1>     pop es
   144 000019E5 BF[D209]            <1>     mov di, el_torito_buf
   145 000019E8 E82EFC              <1>     call ide_read_sectors
   146 000019EB 7279                <1>     jc .fail
   147                              <1> 
   148                              <1>     ; ── Validate Validation Entry ─────────────────────────────────────────
   149                              <1>     ; Byte 0 must be 0x01 (Header ID for Validation Entry).
   150 000019ED 803E[D209]01        <1>     cmp byte [el_torito_buf + 0], 0x01
   151 000019F2 7572                <1>     jne .fail
   152                              <1>     ; Bytes 30–31 must be 0x55, 0xAA (key).
   153 000019F4 803E[F009]55        <1>     cmp byte [el_torito_buf + 30], 0x55
   154 000019F9 756B                <1>     jne .fail
   155 000019FB 803E[F109]AA        <1>     cmp byte [el_torito_buf + 31], 0xAA
   156 00001A00 7564                <1>     jne .fail
   157                              <1> 
   158                              <1>     ; ── Read Initial/Default Entry (bytes 32–63 of the catalog) ──────────
   159                              <1>     ; Byte 32: Boot Indicator — 0x88 = bootable.
   160 00001A02 803E[F209]88        <1>     cmp byte [el_torito_buf + 32], 0x88
   161 00001A07 755D                <1>     jne .fail
   162                              <1> 
   163                              <1>     ; Byte 33: Boot Media Type.
   164 00001A09 A0[F309]            <1>     mov al, [el_torito_buf + 33]
   165 00001A0C 2EA2[1119]          <1>     mov [cs:el_torito_emul], al
   166                              <1> 
   167                              <1>     ; Bytes 34–35: Load Segment (0 → default 0x07C0).
   168 00001A10 A1[F409]            <1>     mov ax, [el_torito_buf + 34]
   169 00001A13 85C0                <1>     test ax, ax
   170 00001A15 7503                <1>     jnz .store_seg
   171 00001A17 B8C007              <1>     mov ax, 0x07C0
   172                              <1> .store_seg:
   173 00001A1A 2EA3[1219]          <1>     mov [cs:el_torito_load_seg], ax
   174                              <1> 
   175                              <1>     ; Bytes 38–39: Sector Count (512-byte virtual sectors to transfer).
   176                              <1>     ; A count of 0 is treated as 1.
   177 00001A1E A1[F809]            <1>     mov ax, [el_torito_buf + 38]
   178 00001A21 85C0                <1>     test ax, ax
   179 00001A23 7503                <1>     jnz .store_count
   180 00001A25 B80100              <1>     mov ax, 1
   181                              <1> .store_count:
   182 00001A28 2EA3[1819]          <1>     mov [cs:el_torito_count], ax
   183                              <1> 
   184                              <1>     ; Bytes 40–43: Load RBA (2048-byte CD sector of the boot image).
   185 00001A2C 66A1[FA09]          <1>     mov eax, [el_torito_buf + 40]
   186 00001A30 2E66A3[1419]        <1>     mov [cs:el_torito_rba], eax
   187                              <1> 
   188                              <1>     ; ── Mark detection as successful ──────────────────────────────────────
   189 00001A35 2EC606[0F19]01      <1>     mov byte [cs:el_torito_present], 1
   190                              <1> 
   191                              <1>     ; Set el_torito_drive based on emulation type.
   192                              <1>     ; All BIOS variables live in the ROM segment (cs:) to avoid conflicts
   193                              <1>     ; with bootloader data in low RAM.
   194 00001A3B 2EA0[1119]          <1>     mov al, [cs:el_torito_emul]
   195 00001A3F 3C04                <1>     cmp al, 0x04
   196 00001A41 740C                <1>     je .drive_hd
   197 00001A43 3C00                <1>     cmp al, 0x00
   198 00001A45 7410                <1>     je .drive_cd
   199                              <1>     ; Floppy emulation (1–3).
   200 00001A47 2EC606[1019]00      <1>     mov byte [cs:el_torito_drive], 0x00
   201 00001A4D EB0E                <1>     jmp .drive_done
   202                              <1> .drive_hd:
   203 00001A4F 2EC606[1019]80      <1>     mov byte [cs:el_torito_drive], 0x80
   204 00001A55 EB06                <1>     jmp .drive_done
   205                              <1> .drive_cd:
   206 00001A57 2EC606[1019]E0      <1>     mov byte [cs:el_torito_drive], 0xE0
   207                              <1> .drive_done:
   208                              <1> 
   209 00001A5D 07                  <1>     pop es
   210 00001A5E 665F                <1>     pop edi
   211 00001A60 6659                <1>     pop ecx
   212 00001A62 6658                <1>     pop eax
   213 00001A64 F8                  <1>     clc
   214 00001A65 C3                  <1>     ret
   215                              <1> 
   216                              <1> .fail:
   217 00001A66 07                  <1>     pop es
   218 00001A67 665F                <1>     pop edi
   219 00001A69 6659                <1>     pop ecx
   220 00001A6B 6658                <1>     pop eax
   221 00001A6D F9                  <1>     stc
   222 00001A6E C3                  <1>     ret
   223                              <1> 
   224                              <1> 
   225                              <1> ; =============================================================================
   226                              <1> ; boot_el_torito
   227                              <1> ;
   228                              <1> ; Loads and executes the El Torito boot image.
   229                              <1> ; Assumes detect_el_torito succeeded and el_torito_* variables are filled.
   230                              <1> ;
   231                              <1> ; The boot image is read from ATA LBA (el_torito_rba × 4) into
   232                              <1> ; (el_torito_load_seg):0x0000.
   233                              <1> ;
   234                              <1> ; DL is set to the appropriate drive number before jumping:
   235                              <1> ;   No emulation (0x00) → DL = 0xE0   (virtual CD-ROM)
   236                              <1> ;   HD  emulation (0x04) → DL = 0x80   (first HD)
   237                              <1> ;   Floppy emulation     → DL = 0x00   (first floppy)
   238                              <1> ;
   239                              <1> ; DS:SI is set to 0x0000:0x0000 (most bootloaders don't require a specific
   240                              <1> ; value; those that query INT 13h AH=4Bh will call it themselves).
   241                              <1> ;
   242                              <1> ; Returns: only on error (CF=1).
   243                              <1> ; =============================================================================
   244                              <1> boot_el_torito:
   245 00001A6F BE[1F01]            <1>     mov si, str_booting_cd
   246 00001A72 E88208              <1>     call bios_print
   247                              <1> 
   248                              <1>     ; Print El Torito parameters for diagnostics.
   249 00001A75 BE[5701]            <1>     mov si, str_et_rba
   250 00001A78 E87C08              <1>     call bios_print
   251 00001A7B 2E66A1[1419]        <1>     mov eax, [cs:el_torito_rba]
   252 00001A80 E8BC08              <1>     call bios_print_hex32
   253 00001A83 BE[5F01]            <1>     mov si, str_et_cnt
   254 00001A86 E86E08              <1>     call bios_print
   255 00001A89 2EA1[1819]          <1>     mov ax, [cs:el_torito_count]
   256 00001A8D E87D08              <1>     call bios_print_dec16
   257 00001A90 BE[6501]            <1>     mov si, str_et_seg
   258 00001A93 E86108              <1>     call bios_print
   259 00001A96 2EA1[1219]          <1>     mov ax, [cs:el_torito_load_seg]
   260 00001A9A E89708              <1>     call bios_print_hex16
   261 00001A9D BE[6B01]            <1>     mov si, str_et_emul
   262 00001AA0 E85408              <1>     call bios_print
   263 00001AA3 2E0FB606[1119]      <1>     movzx ax, byte [cs:el_torito_emul]
   264 00001AA9 E8A208              <1>     call bios_print_hex8
   265 00001AAC BE[7201]            <1>     mov si, str_et_load
   266 00001AAF E84508              <1>     call bios_print
   267                              <1> 
   268                              <1>     ; Convert Load RBA (ISO 2048-byte sector units) to ATA LBA.
   269 00001AB2 2E66A1[1419]        <1>     mov eax, [cs:el_torito_rba]
   270 00001AB7 66C1E002            <1>     shl eax, 2              ; × 4 → ATA LBA
   271                              <1> 
   272                              <1>     ; Number of 512-byte sectors to load.
   273 00001ABB 2E660FB70E[1819]    <1>     movzx ecx, word [cs:el_torito_count]
   274 00001AC2 6685C9              <1>     test ecx, ecx
   275 00001AC5 7506                <1>     jnz .count_ok
   276 00001AC7 66B901000000        <1>     mov ecx, 1
   277                              <1> .count_ok:
   278                              <1> 
   279                              <1>     ; Set ES:DI to (load_seg):0x0000 for the read.
   280 00001ACD 06                  <1>     push es
   281 00001ACE 2E8B1E[1219]        <1>     mov bx, [cs:el_torito_load_seg]
   282 00001AD3 8EC3                <1>     mov es, bx
   283 00001AD5 BF0000              <1>     mov di, 0x0000
   284 00001AD8 E83EFB              <1>     call ide_read_sectors
   285 00001ADB 07                  <1>     pop es
   286 00001ADC 7230                <1>     jc .fail
   287                              <1> 
   288 00001ADE BE[8C01]            <1>     mov si, str_et_loaded
   289 00001AE1 E81308              <1>     call bios_print
   290                              <1> 
   291                              <1>     ; ── Choose DL based on emulation type ─────────────────────────────────
   292 00001AE4 2EA0[1119]          <1>     mov al, [cs:el_torito_emul]
   293 00001AE8 3C04                <1>     cmp al, 0x04
   294 00001AEA 7408                <1>     je .hd_emul
   295 00001AEC 3C00                <1>     cmp al, 0x00
   296 00001AEE 7408                <1>     je .no_emul
   297                              <1>     ; Floppy emulation (media type 1, 2, or 3).
   298 00001AF0 B200                <1>     mov dl, 0x00
   299 00001AF2 EB06                <1>     jmp .jump
   300                              <1> 
   301                              <1> .hd_emul:
   302 00001AF4 B280                <1>     mov dl, 0x80
   303 00001AF6 EB02                <1>     jmp .jump
   304                              <1> 
   305                              <1> .no_emul:
   306 00001AF8 B2E0                <1>     mov dl, 0xE0
   307                              <1> 
   308                              <1> .jump:
   309                              <1>     ; DS:SI = 0 (boot image will call INT 13h AH=4Bh if it needs the packet).
   310 00001AFA 31C0                <1>     xor ax, ax
   311 00001AFC 8ED8                <1>     mov ds, ax
   312 00001AFE 31F6                <1>     xor si, si
   313                              <1> 
   314                              <1>     ; Mark that we are handing off to CD boot image.
   315 00001B00 2EC606[1A19]01      <1>     mov byte [cs:el_torito_handoff], 1
   316                              <1> 
   317                              <1>     ; Far-jump to (load_seg):0x0000 via a far return.
   318                              <1>     ; Stack layout for retf: [SP+0]=offset(IP), [SP+2]=segment(CS).
   319 00001B06 2EA1[1219]          <1>     mov ax, [cs:el_torito_load_seg]
   320 00001B0A 50                  <1>     push ax             ; CS
   321 00001B0B 6A00                <1>     push word 0x0000    ; IP
   322 00001B0D CB                  <1>     retf                ; CS:IP ← load_seg:0x0000
   323                              <1> 
   324                              <1> .fail:
   325 00001B0E F9                  <1>     stc
   326 00001B0F C3                  <1>     ret
    30                                  %include "int1ah.asm"
     1                              <1> ; =============================================================================
     2                              <1> ; int1ah.asm — INT 1Ah: PCI BIOS services + RTC time/date
     3                              <1> ; =============================================================================
     4                              <1> 
     5                              <1> CMOS_ADDR   equ 0x70
     6                              <1> CMOS_DATA   equ 0x71
     7                              <1> 
     8                              <1> int1ah_handler:
     9 00001B10 80FC00              <1>     cmp ah, 0x00
    10 00001B13 7426                <1>     je .get_tick_count
    11 00001B15 80FC01              <1>     cmp ah, 0x01
    12 00001B18 7439                <1>     je .set_tick_count
    13 00001B1A 80FC02              <1>     cmp ah, 0x02
    14 00001B1D 7449                <1>     je .get_rtc_time
    15 00001B1F 80FC03              <1>     cmp ah, 0x03
    16 00001B22 0F848100            <1>     je .set_rtc_time
    17 00001B26 80FC04              <1>     cmp ah, 0x04
    18 00001B29 747E                <1>     je .get_rtc_date
    19 00001B2B 80FC05              <1>     cmp ah, 0x05
    20 00001B2E 0F84C900            <1>     je .set_rtc_date
    21 00001B32 80FCB1              <1>     cmp ah, 0xB1
    22 00001B35 0F84D200            <1>     je .pci_bios
    23                              <1>     ; Unsupported.
    24 00001B39 F9                  <1>     stc
    25 00001B3A CF                  <1>     iret
    26                              <1> 
    27                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    28                              <1> ; AH=00h: Get system timer tick count.
    29                              <1> ;   Returns: CX:DX = tick count, AL = midnight flag.
    30                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    31                              <1> .get_tick_count:
    32 00001B3B 1E                  <1>     push ds
    33 00001B3C 31C0                <1>     xor ax, ax
    34 00001B3E 8ED8                <1>     mov ds, ax
    35 00001B40 8B166C04            <1>     mov dx, [BDA_TIMER_COUNT]
    36 00001B44 8B0E6E04            <1>     mov cx, [BDA_TIMER_COUNT + 2]
    37 00001B48 A07004              <1>     mov al, [BDA_TIMER_OVERFLOW]
    38 00001B4B C606700400          <1>     mov byte [BDA_TIMER_OVERFLOW], 0
    39 00001B50 1F                  <1>     pop ds
    40 00001B51 F8                  <1>     clc
    41 00001B52 CF                  <1>     iret
    42                              <1> 
    43                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    44                              <1> ; AH=01h: Set system timer tick count.
    45                              <1> ;   CX:DX = new tick count.
    46                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    47                              <1> .set_tick_count:
    48 00001B53 1E                  <1>     push ds
    49 00001B54 31C0                <1>     xor ax, ax
    50 00001B56 8ED8                <1>     mov ds, ax
    51 00001B58 89166C04            <1>     mov [BDA_TIMER_COUNT], dx
    52 00001B5C 890E6E04            <1>     mov [BDA_TIMER_COUNT + 2], cx
    53 00001B60 C606700400          <1>     mov byte [BDA_TIMER_OVERFLOW], 0
    54 00001B65 1F                  <1>     pop ds
    55 00001B66 F8                  <1>     clc
    56 00001B67 CF                  <1>     iret
    57                              <1> 
    58                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    59                              <1> ; AH=02h: Get RTC time.
    60                              <1> ;   Returns: CH = hours (BCD), CL = minutes (BCD), DH = seconds (BCD).
    61                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    62                              <1> .get_rtc_time:
    63                              <1>     ; Read CMOS status register B to check BCD vs binary mode.
    64 00001B68 B00B                <1>     mov al, 0x0B
    65 00001B6A E670                <1>     out CMOS_ADDR, al
    66 00001B6C E471                <1>     in al, CMOS_DATA
    67 00001B6E 50                  <1>     push ax                     ; Save status B
    68                              <1> 
    69                              <1>     ; Read seconds.
    70 00001B6F B000                <1>     mov al, 0x00
    71 00001B71 E670                <1>     out CMOS_ADDR, al
    72 00001B73 E471                <1>     in al, CMOS_DATA
    73 00001B75 88C6                <1>     mov dh, al                  ; Seconds
    74                              <1> 
    75                              <1>     ; Read minutes.
    76 00001B77 B002                <1>     mov al, 0x02
    77 00001B79 E670                <1>     out CMOS_ADDR, al
    78 00001B7B E471                <1>     in al, CMOS_DATA
    79 00001B7D 88C1                <1>     mov cl, al                  ; Minutes
    80                              <1> 
    81                              <1>     ; Read hours.
    82 00001B7F B004                <1>     mov al, 0x04
    83 00001B81 E670                <1>     out CMOS_ADDR, al
    84 00001B83 E471                <1>     in al, CMOS_DATA
    85 00001B85 88C5                <1>     mov ch, al                  ; Hours
    86                              <1> 
    87                              <1>     ; Convert binary to BCD if CMOS is in binary mode (bit 2 of status B).
    88 00001B87 58                  <1>     pop ax
    89 00001B88 A804                <1>     test al, 0x04
    90 00001B8A 7415                <1>     jz .rtc_time_done           ; Already BCD
    91                              <1> 
    92                              <1>     ; Convert binary to BCD.
    93 00001B8C 88F0                <1>     mov al, dh
    94 00001B8E E86C00              <1>     call .bin_to_bcd
    95 00001B91 88C6                <1>     mov dh, al
    96 00001B93 88C8                <1>     mov al, cl
    97 00001B95 E86500              <1>     call .bin_to_bcd
    98 00001B98 88C1                <1>     mov cl, al
    99 00001B9A 88E8                <1>     mov al, ch
   100 00001B9C E85E00              <1>     call .bin_to_bcd
   101 00001B9F 88C5                <1>     mov ch, al
   102                              <1> 
   103                              <1> .rtc_time_done:
   104 00001BA1 B200                <1>     mov dl, 0                   ; Daylight saving: off
   105 00001BA3 30E4                <1>     xor ah, ah
   106 00001BA5 F8                  <1>     clc
   107 00001BA6 CF                  <1>     iret
   108                              <1> 
   109                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   110                              <1> ; AH=03h: Set RTC time.
   111                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   112                              <1> .set_rtc_time:
   113                              <1>     ; Not implemented for now.
   114 00001BA7 F8                  <1>     clc
   115 00001BA8 CF                  <1>     iret
   116                              <1> 
   117                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   118                              <1> ; AH=04h: Get RTC date.
   119                              <1> ;   Returns: CH = century (BCD), CL = year (BCD), DH = month (BCD),
   120                              <1> ;            DL = day (BCD).
   121                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   122                              <1> .get_rtc_date:
   123                              <1>     ; Read CMOS status B.
   124 00001BA9 B00B                <1>     mov al, 0x0B
   125 00001BAB E670                <1>     out CMOS_ADDR, al
   126 00001BAD E471                <1>     in al, CMOS_DATA
   127 00001BAF 50                  <1>     push ax
   128                              <1> 
   129                              <1>     ; Day of month.
   130 00001BB0 B007                <1>     mov al, 0x07
   131 00001BB2 E670                <1>     out CMOS_ADDR, al
   132 00001BB4 E471                <1>     in al, CMOS_DATA
   133 00001BB6 88C2                <1>     mov dl, al
   134                              <1> 
   135                              <1>     ; Month.
   136 00001BB8 B008                <1>     mov al, 0x08
   137 00001BBA E670                <1>     out CMOS_ADDR, al
   138 00001BBC E471                <1>     in al, CMOS_DATA
   139 00001BBE 88C6                <1>     mov dh, al
   140                              <1> 
   141                              <1>     ; Year (2-digit).
   142 00001BC0 B009                <1>     mov al, 0x09
   143 00001BC2 E670                <1>     out CMOS_ADDR, al
   144 00001BC4 E471                <1>     in al, CMOS_DATA
   145 00001BC6 88C1                <1>     mov cl, al
   146                              <1> 
   147                              <1>     ; Century (reg 0x32, if available — default to 0x20).
   148 00001BC8 B032                <1>     mov al, 0x32
   149 00001BCA E670                <1>     out CMOS_ADDR, al
   150 00001BCC E471                <1>     in al, CMOS_DATA
   151 00001BCE 84C0                <1>     test al, al
   152 00001BD0 7502                <1>     jnz .have_century
   153 00001BD2 B020                <1>     mov al, 0x20                ; Default: 20xx
   154                              <1> .have_century:
   155 00001BD4 88C5                <1>     mov ch, al
   156                              <1> 
   157                              <1>     ; Convert if binary mode.
   158 00001BD6 58                  <1>     pop ax
   159 00001BD7 A804                <1>     test al, 0x04
   160 00001BD9 741C                <1>     jz .rtc_date_done
   161                              <1> 
   162 00001BDB 88D0                <1>     mov al, dl
   163 00001BDD E81D00              <1>     call .bin_to_bcd
   164 00001BE0 88C2                <1>     mov dl, al
   165 00001BE2 88F0                <1>     mov al, dh
   166 00001BE4 E81600              <1>     call .bin_to_bcd
   167 00001BE7 88C6                <1>     mov dh, al
   168 00001BE9 88C8                <1>     mov al, cl
   169 00001BEB E80F00              <1>     call .bin_to_bcd
   170 00001BEE 88C1                <1>     mov cl, al
   171 00001BF0 88E8                <1>     mov al, ch
   172 00001BF2 E80800              <1>     call .bin_to_bcd
   173 00001BF5 88C5                <1>     mov ch, al
   174                              <1> 
   175                              <1> .rtc_date_done:
   176 00001BF7 30E4                <1>     xor ah, ah
   177 00001BF9 F8                  <1>     clc
   178 00001BFA CF                  <1>     iret
   179                              <1> 
   180                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   181                              <1> ; AH=05h: Set RTC date.
   182                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   183                              <1> .set_rtc_date:
   184 00001BFB F8                  <1>     clc
   185 00001BFC CF                  <1>     iret
   186                              <1> 
   187                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   188                              <1> ; Binary to BCD conversion. AL = binary value, returns AL = BCD.
   189                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   190                              <1> .bin_to_bcd:
   191 00001BFD 51                  <1>     push cx
   192 00001BFE 30E4                <1>     xor ah, ah
   193 00001C00 B10A                <1>     mov cl, 10
   194 00001C02 F6F1                <1>     div cl                      ; AL = tens, AH = ones
   195 00001C04 C0E004              <1>     shl al, 4
   196 00001C07 08E0                <1>     or al, ah
   197 00001C09 59                  <1>     pop cx
   198 00001C0A C3                  <1>     ret
   199                              <1> 
   200                              <1> ; =============================================================================
   201                              <1> ; PCI BIOS services (AH=B1h)
   202                              <1> ; =============================================================================
   203                              <1> 
   204                              <1> .pci_bios:
   205 00001C0B 3C01                <1>     cmp al, 0x01
   206 00001C0D 7432                <1>     je .pci_present
   207 00001C0F 3C02                <1>     cmp al, 0x02
   208 00001C11 7440                <1>     je .pci_find_device
   209 00001C13 3C03                <1>     cmp al, 0x03
   210 00001C15 0F848900            <1>     je .pci_find_class
   211 00001C19 3C08                <1>     cmp al, 0x08
   212 00001C1B 0F84DE00            <1>     je .pci_read_byte
   213 00001C1F 3C09                <1>     cmp al, 0x09
   214 00001C21 0F84FF00            <1>     je .pci_read_word
   215 00001C25 3C0A                <1>     cmp al, 0x0A
   216 00001C27 0F841E01            <1>     je .pci_read_dword
   217 00001C2B 3C0B                <1>     cmp al, 0x0B
   218 00001C2D 0F843401            <1>     je .pci_write_byte
   219 00001C31 3C0C                <1>     cmp al, 0x0C
   220 00001C33 0F847F01            <1>     je .pci_write_word
   221 00001C37 3C0D                <1>     cmp al, 0x0D
   222 00001C39 0F84BA01            <1>     je .pci_write_dword
   223                              <1>     ; Unknown PCI function.
   224 00001C3D B481                <1>     mov ah, 0x81                ; Function not supported
   225 00001C3F F9                  <1>     stc
   226 00001C40 CF                  <1>     iret
   227                              <1> 
   228                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   229                              <1> ; AX=B101h: PCI BIOS present check.
   230                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   231                              <1> .pci_present:
   232 00001C41 66BA50434920        <1>     mov edx, 0x20494350        ; "PCI "
   233 00001C47 B400                <1>     mov ah, 0x00                ; Success
   234 00001C49 B702                <1>     mov bh, 0x02                ; PCI revision 2.1 major
   235 00001C4B B310                <1>     mov bl, 0x10                ; PCI revision 2.1 minor
   236 00001C4D B100                <1>     mov cl, 0x00                ; Max bus number
   237 00001C4F B001                <1>     mov al, 0x01                ; Hardware mechanism 1
   238 00001C51 F8                  <1>     clc
   239 00001C52 CF                  <1>     iret
   240                              <1> 
   241                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   242                              <1> ; AX=B102h: Find PCI device by vendor/device ID.
   243                              <1> ;   CX = device ID, DX = vendor ID, SI = index (0 = first match).
   244                              <1> ;   Returns: BH = bus, BL = dev/func.
   245                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   246                              <1> .pci_find_device:
   247 00001C53 1E                  <1>     push ds
   248 00001C54 57                  <1>     push di
   249 00001C55 50                  <1>     push ax
   250                              <1> 
   251 00001C56 B800F0              <1>     mov ax, 0xF000

    30                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    31                              <1> ; INT 09h: Keyboard interrupt handler (IRQ 1).
    32                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    33                              <1> keyboard_irq_handler:
    34 00001E63 1E                  <1>     push ds
    35 00001E64 50                  <1>     push ax
    36 00001E65 53                  <1>     push bx
    37 00001E66 51                  <1>     push cx
    38                              <1> 
    39 00001E67 31C0                <1>     xor ax, ax
    40 00001E69 8ED8                <1>     mov ds, ax
    41                              <1> 
    42                              <1>     ; Read scancode from PS/2 controller.
    43 00001E6B E460                <1>     in al, 0x60
    44                              <1> 
    45                              <1>     ; Ignore break codes (key release, bit 7 set).
    46 00001E6D A880                <1>     test al, 0x80
    47 00001E6F 7534                <1>     jnz .eoi
    48                              <1> 
    49                              <1>     ; Translate scancode to ASCII via table in BIOS segment (CS: override).
    50 00001E71 0FB6D8              <1>     movzx bx, al               ; BX = scancode index
    51 00001E74 83FB54              <1>     cmp bx, scancode_table_size
    52 00001E77 732C                <1>     jae .eoi
    53                              <1> 
    54 00001E79 88C4                <1>     mov ah, al                  ; AH = scan code
    55 00001E7B 2E8A87[AE1E]        <1>     mov al, [cs:scancode_table + bx]    ; AL = ASCII
    56                              <1> 
    57                              <1>     ; If ASCII is 0, skip (non-printable/unmapped key).
    58 00001E80 84C0                <1>     test al, al
    59 00001E82 7421                <1>     jz .eoi
    60                              <1> 
    61                              <1>     ; Store in keyboard buffer (AH=scancode, AL=ASCII).
    62 00001E84 8B1E1C04            <1>     mov bx, [BDA_KBD_BUF_TAIL]
    63 00001E88 89D9                <1>     mov cx, bx
    64 00001E8A 83C102              <1>     add cx, 2
    65 00001E8D 3B0E8204            <1>     cmp cx, [BDA_KBD_BUF_END]
    66 00001E91 7204                <1>     jb .no_wrap
    67 00001E93 8B0E8004            <1>     mov cx, [BDA_KBD_BUF_START]
    68                              <1> .no_wrap:
    69 00001E97 3B0E1A04            <1>     cmp cx, [BDA_KBD_BUF_HEAD]
    70 00001E9B 7408                <1>     je .eoi                     ; Buffer full
    71                              <1> 
    72 00001E9D 89870004            <1>     mov [bx + 0x0400], ax
    73 00001EA1 890E1C04            <1>     mov [BDA_KBD_BUF_TAIL], cx
    74                              <1> 
    75                              <1> .eoi:
    76 00001EA5 B020                <1>     mov al, 0x20
    77 00001EA7 E620                <1>     out PIC1_CMD, al
    78                              <1> 
    79 00001EA9 59                  <1>     pop cx
    80 00001EAA 5B                  <1>     pop bx
    81 00001EAB 58                  <1>     pop ax
    82 00001EAC 1F                  <1>     pop ds
    83 00001EAD CF                  <1>     iret
    84                              <1> 
    85                              <1> ; Scancode set 1 to ASCII translation table (US keyboard layout).
    86                              <1> scancode_table:
    87 00001EAE 00                  <1>     db 0                        ; 0x00: none
    88 00001EAF 1B                  <1>     db 27                       ; 0x01: Escape
    89 00001EB0 313233343536373839- <1>     db '1','2','3','4','5','6','7','8','9','0'  ; 0x02-0x0B
    89 00001EB9 30                  <1>
    90 00001EBA 2D3D                <1>     db '-','='                  ; 0x0C-0x0D
    91 00001EBC 08                  <1>     db 8                        ; 0x0E: Backspace
    92 00001EBD 09                  <1>     db 9                        ; 0x0F: Tab
    93 00001EBE 71776572747975696F- <1>     db 'q','w','e','r','t','y','u','i','o','p'  ; 0x10-0x19
    93 00001EC7 70                  <1>
    94 00001EC8 5B5D                <1>     db '[',']'                  ; 0x1A-0x1B
    95 00001ECA 0D                  <1>     db 13                       ; 0x1C: Enter
    96 00001ECB 00                  <1>     db 0                        ; 0x1D: Left Ctrl
    97 00001ECC 6173646667686A6B6C  <1>     db 'a','s','d','f','g','h','j','k','l'      ; 0x1E-0x26
    98 00001ED5 3B27                <1>     db ';',"'"                  ; 0x27-0x28
    99 00001ED7 60                  <1>     db '`'                      ; 0x29: Backtick
   100 00001ED8 00                  <1>     db 0                        ; 0x2A: Left Shift

   110 00001EF3 00<rep Fh>          <1>     times 15 db 0               ; 0x45-0x53: misc keys

   242 0000212B 6631DB              <1>     xor ebx, ebx               ; Start at entry 0
   243 0000212E 66BA50414D53        <1>     mov edx, 0x534D4150         ; 'SMAP'
   244 00002134 66B918000000        <1>     mov ecx, 24
   245 0000213A 66B820E80000        <1>     mov eax, 0xE820
   246 00002140 CD15                <1>     int 0x15
   247                              <1> 
   248 00002142 7246                <1>     jc .e820_fail
   249 00002144 663D50414D53        <1>     cmp eax, 0x534D4150         ; Signature returned?
   250 0000214A 753E                <1>     jne .e820_fail
   251                              <1> 
   252                              <1>     ; Count entries by iterating.
   253 0000214C 31C9                <1>     xor cx, cx
   254 0000214E 41                  <1>     inc cx                      ; Already got entry 0
   255                              <1> .e820_loop:
   256 0000214F 6685DB              <1>     test ebx, ebx
   257 00002152 741C                <1>     jz .e820_done               ; EBX=0 means last entry
   258 00002154 BF0006              <1>     mov di, 0x0600
   259 00002157 66BA50414D53        <1>     mov edx, 0x534D4150
   260 0000215D 66B918000000        <1>     mov ecx, 24
   261 00002163 66B820E80000        <1>     mov eax, 0xE820
   262 00002169 CD15                <1>     int 0x15
   263 0000216B 7203                <1>     jc .e820_done
   264 0000216D 41                  <1>     inc cx
   265 0000216E EBDF                <1>     jmp .e820_loop
   266                              <1> 
   267                              <1> .e820_done:
   268                              <1>     ; CX = number of entries. Should be >= 4 (conv + EBDA + ROM + ext).
   269 00002170 83F904              <1>     cmp cx, 4
   270 00002173 7215                <1>     jb .e820_fail
   271                              <1> 
   272 00002175 51                  <1>     push cx
   273 00002176 E8A8FE              <1>     call selftest_print_ok
   274 00002179 BE[A003]            <1>     mov si, str_st_lparen
   275 0000217C E87801              <1>     call bios_print
   276 0000217F 58                  <1>     pop ax
   277 00002180 E88A01              <1>     call bios_print_dec16
   278 00002183 BE[B003]            <1>     mov si, str_st_entries
   279 00002186 E86E01              <1>     call bios_print
   280 00002189 C3                  <1>     ret
   281                              <1> .e820_fail:

   283 0000218D C3                  <1>     ret

   296                              <1>     ; AL should be 1 (A20 enabled) in our hypervisor.
   297 0000219B 3C01                <1>     cmp al, 1
   298 0000219D 7504                <1>     jne .a20_fail
   299                              <1> 
   300 0000219F E87FFE              <1>     call selftest_print_ok
   301 000021A2 C3                  <1>     ret

   304 000021A6 C3                  <1>     ret

   438                              <1> ; ===========================================================================
   439                              <1> ; Test 10: Timer tick — read BDA timer, wait briefly, verify it increments.
   440                              <1> ; ===========================================================================
   441                              <1> selftest_timer:
   442 0000228B BE[6E03]            <1>     mov si, str_st_timer
   443 0000228E E86600              <1>     call bios_print
   444                              <1> 
   445                              <1>     ; Read initial tick count.
   446 00002291 B400                <1>     mov ah, 0x00
   447 00002293 CD1A                <1>     int 0x1A                    ; CX:DX = tick count
   448 00002295 89D3                <1>     mov bx, dx                  ; Save low word
   449                              <1> 
   450                              <1>     ; Spin-wait for tick to change (up to ~64K iterations).
   451 00002297 B9FFFF              <1>     mov cx, 0xFFFF
   452                              <1> .timer_wait:
   453 0000229A 51                  <1>     push cx
   454 0000229B B400                <1>     mov ah, 0x00
   455 0000229D CD1A                <1>     int 0x1A
   456 0000229F 59                  <1>     pop cx
   457 000022A0 39DA                <1>     cmp dx, bx                  ; Changed?
   458 000022A2 7507                <1>     jne .timer_ok
   459 000022A4 49                  <1>     dec cx
   460 000022A5 75F3                <1>     jnz .timer_wait
   461                              <1> 
   462                              <1>     ; Timed out — timer did not tick.
   463 000022A7 E888FD              <1>     call selftest_print_fail
   464 000022AA C3                  <1>     ret
   465                              <1> .timer_ok:
   466 000022AB E873FD              <1>     call selftest_print_ok
   467 000022AE C3                  <1>     ret
   468                              <1> 
   469                              <1> ; ===========================================================================
   470                              <1> ; Test 11: IVT integrity — check key interrupt vectors are non-zero.
   471                              <1> ; ===========================================================================

   473 000022AF BE[8703]            <1>     mov si, str_st_ivt

   475                              <1>

   481 000022BD 6685C0              <1>     test eax, eax
   482 000022C0 7431                <1>     jz .ivt_fail

   487 000022C9 7428                <1>     jz .ivt_fail
   488                              <1>

   490 000022CB 66A15400            <1>     mov eax, [0x15 * 4]
   491 000022CF 6685C0              <1>     test eax, eax
   492 000022D2 741F                <1>     jz .ivt_fail
   493                              <1>

   496 000022D8 6685C0              <1>     test eax, eax
   497 000022DB 7416                <1>     jz .ivt_fail
   498                              <1>

   500 000022DD 66A16400            <1>     mov eax, [0x19 * 4]

   502 000022E4 740D                <1>     jz .ivt_fail
   503                              <1>

   505 000022E6 66A16800            <1>     mov eax, [0x1A * 4]

   511                              <1> .ivt_fail:

   513 000022F6 C3                  <1>     ret
    33                                  %include "boot.asm"
     1                              <1> ; =============================================================================
     2                              <1> ; boot.asm — Boot device helpers and bios_print utility
     3                              <1> ; =============================================================================
     4                              <1> 
     5                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
     6                              <1> ; bios_print — Print NUL-terminated string via INT 10h TTY.
     7                              <1> ;   Input: DS:SI = string pointer (caller must set DS = 0xF000 if needed).
     8                              <1> ;
     9                              <1> ;   This function uses direct VGA writes instead of INT 10h to avoid
    10                              <1> ;   recursion issues during early POST (before IVT is set up).
    11                              <1> ;   After IVT setup, callers can also use INT 10h directly.
    12                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    13                              <1> bios_print:
    14 000022F7 50                  <1>     push ax
    15 000022F8 53                  <1>     push bx
    16 000022F9 56                  <1>     push si
    17                              <1> 
    18                              <1> .print_loop:
    19                              <1>     ; Use CS: override to read from BIOS segment.
    20 000022FA 2EAC                <1>     cs lodsb
    21 000022FC 84C0                <1>     test al, al
    22 000022FE 7409                <1>     jz .print_done
    23 00002300 B40E                <1>     mov ah, 0x0E
    24 00002302 BB0700              <1>     mov bx, 0x0007             ; Page 0, light gray
    25 00002305 CD10                <1>     int 0x10
    26 00002307 EBF1                <1>     jmp .print_loop
    27                              <1> 
    28                              <1> .print_done:
    29 00002309 5E                  <1>     pop si
    30 0000230A 5B                  <1>     pop bx
    31 0000230B 58                  <1>     pop ax
    32 0000230C C3                  <1>     ret
    33                              <1> 
    34                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    35                              <1> ; bios_print_dec16 — Print a 16-bit unsigned decimal number.
    36                              <1> ;   Input: AX = number to print.
    37                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    38                              <1> bios_print_dec16:
    39 0000230D 50                  <1>     push ax
    40 0000230E 53                  <1>     push bx
    41 0000230F 51                  <1>     push cx
    42 00002310 52                  <1>     push dx
    43                              <1> 
    44 00002311 31C9                <1>     xor cx, cx                  ; Digit count
    45 00002313 BB0A00              <1>     mov bx, 10
    46                              <1> 
    47                              <1> .dec_div:
    48 00002316 31D2                <1>     xor dx, dx
    49 00002318 F7F3                <1>     div bx                     ; AX = quotient, DX = remainder
    50 0000231A 52                  <1>     push dx                     ; Save digit
    51 0000231B 41                  <1>     inc cx
    52 0000231C 85C0                <1>     test ax, ax
    53 0000231E 75F6                <1>     jnz .dec_div
    54                              <1> 
    55                              <1> .dec_print:
    56 00002320 58                  <1>     pop ax
    57 00002321 0430                <1>     add al, '0'
    58 00002323 B40E                <1>     mov ah, 0x0E
    59 00002325 53                  <1>     push bx
    60 00002326 BB0700              <1>     mov bx, 0x0007
    61 00002329 CD10                <1>     int 0x10
    62 0000232B 5B                  <1>     pop bx
    63 0000232C 49                  <1>     dec cx
    64 0000232D 75F1                <1>     jnz .dec_print
    65                              <1> 
    66 0000232F 5A                  <1>     pop dx
    67 00002330 59                  <1>     pop cx
    68 00002331 5B                  <1>     pop bx
    69 00002332 58                  <1>     pop ax
    70 00002333 C3                  <1>     ret
    71                              <1> 
    72                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    73                              <1> ; bios_print_hex16 — Print a 16-bit hex value (4 hex digits).
    74                              <1> ;   Input: AX = value.
    75                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    76                              <1> bios_print_hex16:
    77 00002334 50                  <1>     push ax
    78 00002335 88E0                <1>     mov al, ah          ; High byte first
    79 00002337 E81400              <1>     call bios_print_hex8
    80 0000233A 58                  <1>     pop ax
    81 0000233B E81000              <1>     call bios_print_hex8  ; Low byte (AL from original AX)
    82 0000233E C3                  <1>     ret
    83                              <1> 
    84                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    85                              <1> ; bios_print_hex32 — Print a 32-bit hex value (8 hex digits).
    86                              <1> ;   Input: EAX = value.
    87                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    88                              <1> bios_print_hex32:
    89 0000233F 6650                <1>     push eax
    90 00002341 66C1E810            <1>     shr eax, 16
    91 00002345 E8ECFF              <1>     call bios_print_hex16   ; High 16 bits
    92 00002348 6658                <1>     pop eax
    93 0000234A E8E7FF              <1>     call bios_print_hex16   ; Low 16 bits (AX = lower 16 of original EAX)
    94 0000234D C3                  <1>     ret
    95                              <1> 
    96                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    97                              <1> ; bios_print_hex8 — Print an 8-bit hex value.
    98                              <1> ;   Input: AL = value.
    99                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   100                              <1> bios_print_hex8:
   101 0000234E 50                  <1>     push ax
   102 0000234F 53                  <1>     push bx
   103 00002350 51                  <1>     push cx
   104                              <1> 
   105 00002351 88C1                <1>     mov cl, al
   106 00002353 C0E804              <1>     shr al, 4
   107 00002356 E80B00              <1>     call .hex_nibble
   108 00002359 88C8                <1>     mov al, cl
   109 0000235B 240F                <1>     and al, 0x0F
   110 0000235D E80400              <1>     call .hex_nibble
   111                              <1> 
   112 00002360 59                  <1>     pop cx
   113 00002361 5B                  <1>     pop bx
   114 00002362 58                  <1>     pop ax
   115 00002363 C3                  <1>     ret
   116                              <1> 
   117                              <1> .hex_nibble:
   118 00002364 3C0A                <1>     cmp al, 10
   119 00002366 7204                <1>     jb .hex_digit
   120 00002368 0437                <1>     add al, 'A' - 10
   121 0000236A EB02                <1>     jmp .hex_out
   122                              <1> .hex_digit:
   123 0000236C 0430                <1>     add al, '0'
   124                              <1> .hex_out:
   125 0000236E B40E                <1>     mov ah, 0x0E
   126 00002370 BB0700              <1>     mov bx, 0x0007
   127 00002373 CD10                <1>     int 0x10
   128 00002375 C3                  <1>     ret
    34                                  %include "ivt.asm"
     1                              <1> ; =============================================================================
     2                              <1> ; ivt.asm — Interrupt Vector Table setup
     3                              <1> ; =============================================================================
     4                              <1> 
     5                              <1> BIOS_SEG    equ 0xF000
     6                              <1> 
     7                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
     8                              <1> ; ivt_setup — Install all interrupt vectors at 0x0000:0x0000.
     9                              <1> ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    10                              <1> ivt_setup:
    11 00002376 06                  <1>     push es
    12 00002377 57                  <1>     push di
    13 00002378 50                  <1>     push ax
    14 00002379 51                  <1>     push cx
    15                              <1> 
    16                              <1>     ; First, fill all 256 vectors with the dummy iret handler.
    17 0000237A 31C0                <1>     xor ax, ax
    18 0000237C 8EC0                <1>     mov es, ax
    19 0000237E 31FF                <1>     xor di, di
    20 00002380 B90001              <1>     mov cx, 256
    21                              <1> .fill_default:
    22 00002383 26C705[701F]        <1>     mov word [es:di], iret_handler
    23 00002388 26C7450200F0        <1>     mov word [es:di + 2], BIOS_SEG
    24 0000238E 83C704              <1>     add di, 4
    25 00002391 E2F0                <1>     loop .fill_default
    26                              <1> 
    27                              <1>     ; Now install specific handlers.
    28                              <1> 
    29                              <1>     ; CPU exceptions 0x00-0x07 → iret_handler (already set).
    30                              <1> 
    31                              <1>     ; IRQ handlers (master PIC: INT 08h-0Fh).
    32 00002393 BF2000              <1>     mov di, 0x08 * 4
    33 00002396 26C705[381E]        <1>     mov word [es:di], timer_tick_handler
    34 0000239B 26C7450200F0        <1>     mov word [es:di + 2], BIOS_SEG
    35                              <1> 
    36 000023A1 BF2400              <1>     mov di, 0x09 * 4
    37 000023A4 26C705[631E]        <1>     mov word [es:di], keyboard_irq_handler
    38 000023A9 26C7450200F0        <1>     mov word [es:di + 2], BIOS_SEG
    39                              <1> 
    40                              <1>     ; IRQ 2 (cascade) → eoi_iret.
    41 000023AF BF2800              <1>     mov di, 0x0A * 4
    42 000023B2 26C705[711F]        <1>     mov word [es:di], eoi_iret
    43 000023B7 26C7450200F0        <1>     mov word [es:di + 2], BIOS_SEG
    44                              <1> 
    45                              <1>     ; IRQ 3-7 → eoi_iret.
    46 000023BD B90500              <1>     mov cx, 5
    47 000023C0 BF2C00              <1>     mov di, 0x0B * 4
    48                              <1> .master_irqs:
    49 000023C3 26C705[711F]        <1>     mov word [es:di], eoi_iret
    50 000023C8 26C7450200F0        <1>     mov word [es:di + 2], BIOS_SEG
    51 000023CE 83C704              <1>     add di, 4
    52 000023D1 E2F0                <1>     loop .master_irqs
    53                              <1> 
    54                              <1>     ; BIOS service interrupts.
    55 000023D3 BF4000              <1>     mov di, 0x10 * 4
    56 000023D6 26C705[A50C]        <1>     mov word [es:di], int10h_handler
    57 000023DB 26C7450200F0        <1>     mov word [es:di + 2], BIOS_SEG
    58                              <1> 
    59 000023E1 BF4400              <1>     mov di, 0x11 * 4
    60 000023E4 26C705[021F]        <1>     mov word [es:di], int11h_handler
    61 000023E9 26C7450200F0        <1>     mov word [es:di + 2], BIOS_SEG
    62                              <1> 
    63 000023EF BF4800              <1>     mov di, 0x12 * 4
    64 000023F2 26C705[0C1F]        <1>     mov word [es:di], int12h_handler
    65 000023F7 26C7450200F0        <1>     mov word [es:di + 2], BIOS_SEG
    66                              <1> 
    67 000023FD BF4C00              <1>     mov di, 0x13 * 4
    68 00002400 26C705[4211]        <1>     mov word [es:di], int13h_handler
    69 00002405 26C7450200F0        <1>     mov word [es:di + 2], BIOS_SEG
    70                              <1> 
    71 0000240B BF5000              <1>     mov di, 0x14 * 4
    72 0000240E 26C705[161F]        <1>     mov word [es:di], int14h_handler
    73 00002413 26C7450200F0        <1>     mov word [es:di + 2], BIOS_SEG
    74                              <1> 
    75 00002419 BF5400              <1>     mov di, 0x15 * 4
    76 0000241C 26C705[6517]        <1>     mov word [es:di], int15h_handler
    77 00002421 26C7450200F0        <1>     mov word [es:di + 2], BIOS_SEG
    78                              <1> 
    79 00002427 BF5800              <1>     mov di, 0x16 * 4
    80 0000242A 26C705[7D18]        <1>     mov word [es:di], int16h_handler

     6                              <1> ; post_entry — Main BIOS initialization. Called from reset vector.

    17                              <1>

    37                                  ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    38                                  ; Pad to 0xFFF0 (64KB - 16 bytes), then place the reset vector.
    39                                  ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    40 0000257B FF<rep DA75h>           times (0xFFF0 - ($ - $$)) db 0xFF
    41                                  
    42                                  reset_vector:
    43 0000FFF0 EA[7A24]00F0                jmp 0xF000:post_entry       ; Far jump to POST (5 bytes)
    44 0000FFF5 00                          db 0                        ; Padding
    45 0000FFF6 30332F30332F3236            db '03/03/26'               ; BIOS date string (8 bytes at 0xFFF5)
    46 0000FFFE FF                          db 0xFF                     ; Model byte
    47 0000FFFF 00                          db 0                        ; Checksum placeholder
    48                                  
    49                                  ; Ensure we are exactly 64KB.
    50                                  times (0x10000 - ($ - $$)) db 0xFF
